(function () {
    var kClick = '__lumniPianoTrackingClickBound';
    var kImp = '__lumniPianoTrackingImpressionBound';
    var nativeAdd = Document.prototype.addEventListener;
    Document.prototype.addEventListener = function (type, listener, options) {
        if (this === document && type === 'Tracking:click' && window[kClick]) {
            return;
        }
        if (this === document && type === 'Tracking:impression' && window[kImp]) {
            return;
        }
        if (this === document && type === 'Tracking:click') {
            window[kClick] = true;
        }
        if (this === document && type === 'Tracking:impression') {
            window[kImp] = true;
        }
        return nativeAdd.call(this, type, listener, options);
    };
})();

  (function () {
    var EMAIL_REGEX = /[A-Z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Z0-9.-]+\.[A-Z]{2,}/gi;
    var SENSITIVE_KV_REGEX = /(identifiant|login|user(?:name)?|mail|email|mot[\s_-]*de[\s_-]*passe|password|pass)\s*[:=]\s*[^/&?#]+/gi;

    var sanitizeTrackingUrl = function (rawUrl) {
      if (typeof rawUrl !== 'string' || rawUrl === '') {
        return '';
      }

      try {
        var url = new URL(rawUrl);
        var safePath = decodeURIComponent(url.pathname || '/')
          .replace(EMAIL_REGEX, '[redacted-email]')
          .replace(SENSITIVE_KV_REGEX, '$1:[redacted]');

        return ''.concat(url.origin).concat(safePath);
      } catch (e) {
        return rawUrl
          .split('#')[0]
          .split('?')[0]
          .replace(EMAIL_REGEX, '[redacted-email]')
          .replace(SENSITIVE_KV_REGEX, '$1:[redacted]');
      }
    };


    var fireDatalayer = function () {
      if (window.tC && window.tC.event && typeof window.tC.event.datalayer === 'function') {
        if (window.tC.internalvars && typeof window.location !== 'undefined') {
          window.tC.internalvars.url = sanitizeTrackingUrl(window.location.href);
        }
        window.tC.event.datalayer(this, { 'id': 'datalayer' });
      }
    };
    if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
      document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', fireDatalayer, { once: true });
    } else {
      fireDatalayer();
    }
  })();
